## 3. Top 5 Culturally Sensitive / Nuanced Strings Flagged for Native Review

These 5 strings carry deep philosophical, spiritual, or commercial weight and have been translated with high care. Reviewers should confirm the tone aligns with local spiritual and yoga communities:

| # | Focus Area | English Source | Target Translation | Back-Translation | Linguistic Review Rationale |
| :-: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1** | **1-Year Post-Graduation Mentorship Promise** (`home.whyChoose.postTtcDesc`) | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Kebanyakan sekolah berpamitan pada hari kelulusan. Kami tidak. Selama 12 bulan setelah pelatihan, Anda mendapatkan sesi live bulanan bersama kami — umpan balik untuk kelas nyata Anda jika Anda mengajar, bimbingan untuk latihan pribadi jika Anda tidak mengajar — serta guru yang siap menjawab pesan Anda saat menghadapi kendala. Ini bukan trik pemasaran: murid-murid dari pelatihan masa lalu pendiri kami masih berlatih online bersamanya selama 3, 4, bahkan 5 tahun setelah kursus usai. Kami hanya meresmikan apa yang sudah menjadi kenyataan. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Expresses the school's core 12-month mentorship commitment with monthly live video guidance and personal feedback. |
| **2** | **Student Manifesto / Pure Practice Nuance** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoQuote`) | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | «Saya sebenarnya tidak berniat mengajar. Saya hanya ingin mendalami latihan saya.» | "I don't actually want to teach. I just want to go deeper." | High philosophical nuance distinguishing commercial fitness certification from traditional authentic sadhana transmission. |
| **3** | **Philosophy of Self-Discovery (Non-Career Focus)** (`home.whyChoose.manifestoIntro`) | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Lebih dari separuh orang yang menulis kepada kami mengatakan hal ini, hampir seperti meminta maaf. Tolong jangan meminta maaf. Di India, yoga tidak pernah menjadi kursus kejuruan semata — yoga adalah jalan untuk memahami diri sendiri. Siswa selalu menjadi yang utama. | More than half the people who write to us say this, almost apologetically. Please don't apologize. In India, yoga was never a career course — it was a pathway to understand oneself. The student always came first. | Reframing yoga teacher training as personal self-realization rather than a commercial career course. |
| **4** | **Sacred Vedic Fire Ceremony (Agni Hotra)** (`course200.inclusions.incCeremonies`) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Upacara api pembukaan dan penutupan tradisional (Agni Hotra) | Traditional opening and closing fire ceremonies (Agni Hotra) | Preservation of ancient Vedic sacred fire purification rituals and spiritual tradition. |
| **5** | **Sacred Waterfall Cleansing Ceremony (Melukat)** (`course200.excursionsList[2].description`) | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Menuju air terjun sakral untuk mengikuti ritual penyucian diri tradisional dengan air sumber alami. | Journey to a sacred waterfall for a traditional cleansing ritual. Let the pure waters wash away what no longer serves you. | Balinese Hindu purification ritual at a sacred holy water temple; requires deep cultural respect and sacred phrasing. |

| Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Wondering if you are truly ready for a Yoga Teacher Training Course (TTC)? | Homepage: Why YogaGarhi Pillars | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Kebanyakan sekolah berpamitan pada hari kelulusan. Kami tidak. Selama 12 bulan setelah pelatihan, Anda mendapatkan sesi live bulanan bersama kami — umpan balik untuk kelas nyata Anda jika Anda mengajar, bimbingan untuk latihan pribadi jika Anda tidak mengajar — serta guru yang siap menjawab pesan Anda saat menghadapi kendala. Ini bukan trik pemasaran: murid-murid dari pelatihan masa lalu pendiri kami masih berlatih online bersamanya selama 3, 4, bahkan 5 tahun setelah kursus usai. Kami hanya meresmikan apa yang sudah menjadi kenyataan. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| 200hr Bali: faqs[2] | A typical training day at YogaGarhi follows our verified daily schedule: 6:00 AM Wake Up & Morning Cleansing; 6:30 AM Pranayama & Meditation; 7:30 AM Hatha & Vinyasa; 9:00 AM Breakfast; 10:00 AM Yoga Philosophy; 12:00 PM Lunch & Rest; 3:00 PM Applied Anatomy & Bio Mechanics; 4:30 PM Alignment, Adjustments & Teaching Methodology; 6:00 PM Ashtanga & Iyengar with Yoga Therapy; 7:30 PM Dinner; 9:00 PM Self-Study / Lights Off. | Hari pelatihan di YogaGarhi mengikuti jadwal terstruktur: 06:00 Bangun; 06:30 Pranayama & Meditasi; 07:30 Hatha & Vinyasa; 09:00 Sarapan; 10:00 Filosofi Yoga; 12:00 Makan Siang & Istirahat; 15:00 Anatomi Terapan; 16:30 Penjajaran & Metodologi; 18:00 Ashtanga & Iyengar bersama terapi yoga; 19:30 Makan Malam; 21:00 Belajar Mandiri & Jam Malam. | A typical training day at YogaGarhi follows our verified daily schedule: 6:00 AM Wake Up & Morning Cleansing; 6:30 AM Pranayama & Meditation; 7:30 AM Hatha & Vinyasa; 9:00 AM Breakfast; 10:00 AM Yoga Philosophy; 12:00 PM Lunch & Rest; 3:00 PM Applied Anatomy & Bio Mechanics; 4:30 PM Alignment, Adjustments & Teaching Methodology; 6:00 PM Ashtanga & Iyengar with Yoga Therapy; 7:30 PM Dinner; 9:00 PM Self-Study / Lights Off. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within ID grammatical structure. |

| 200hr Bali: overview | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Pelatihan Guru Yoga 200 Jam kami di Bali adalah kursus menyeluruh tingkat pemula hingga menengah bagi siapa saja yang ingin menjadi guru yoga percaya diri atau memperdalam yoga dalam kehidupan sehari-hari. YogaGarhi berada di Gianyar, sekitar 15 km dari Ubud, menawarkan ketenangan alam pedesaan. Selama kursus, Anda mempraktikkan asana setiap hari, mempelajari anatomi penjajaran, pranayama, meditasi, filosofi yoga, dan metode mengajar. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within ID grammatical structure. |

| Why Choose YogaGarhi | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Kebanyakan sekolah berpamitan pada hari kelulusan. Kami tidak. Selama 12 bulan setelah pelatihan, Anda mendapatkan sesi live bulanan bersama kami — umpan balik untuk kelas nyata Anda jika Anda mengajar, bimbingan untuk latihan pribadi jika Anda tidak mengajar — serta guru yang siap menjawab pesan Anda saat menghadapi kendala. Ini bukan trik pemasaran: murid-murid dari pelatihan masa lalu pendiri kami masih berlatih online bersamanya selama 3, 4, bahkan 5 tahun setelah kursus usai. Kami hanya meresmikan apa yang sudah menjadi kenyataan. | Most schools say goodbye on graduation day. We don't. For 12 months after your training, you get monthly live sessions with us — feedback on your real classes if you teach, guidance on your personal practice if you don't — and a teacher you can write to when you're stuck. This isn't a new idea we invented for marketing: students from our founder's past trainings have been practicing with him online for three, four, even five years after their course ended. We simply made into a promise what was already true. | Verified accurate. Natural native phrasing. |

| 100hr Bali YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Pelatihan Guru Yoga 200 Jam kami di Bali adalah kursus menyeluruh tingkat pemula hingga menengah bagi siapa saja yang ingin menjadi guru yoga percaya diri atau memperdalam yoga dalam kehidupan sehari-hari. YogaGarhi berada di Gianyar, sekitar 15 km dari Ubud, menawarkan ketenangan alam pedesaan. Selama kursus, Anda mempraktikkan asana setiap hari, mempelajari anatomi penjajaran, pranayama, meditasi, filosofi yoga, dan metode mengajar. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. | 300hr Bali Advanced YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Pelatihan Guru Yoga 200 Jam kami di Bali adalah kursus menyeluruh tingkat pemula hingga menengah bagi siapa saja yang ingin menjadi guru yoga percaya diri atau memperdalam yoga dalam kehidupan sehari-hari. YogaGarhi berada di Gianyar, sekitar 15 km dari Ubud, menawarkan ketenangan alam pedesaan. Selama kursus, Anda mempraktikkan asana setiap hari, mempelajari anatomi penjajaran, pranayama, meditasi, filosofi yoga, dan metode mengajar. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within ID grammatical structure. |

| 100hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Pelatihan Guru Yoga 200 Jam kami di Bali adalah kursus menyeluruh tingkat pemula hingga menengah bagi siapa saja yang ingin menjadi guru yoga percaya diri atau memperdalam yoga dalam kehidupan sehari-hari. YogaGarhi berada di Gianyar, sekitar 15 km dari Ubud, menawarkan ketenangan alam pedesaan. | 200hr Rishikesh YTTC | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. During the training, you practice asanas every day, learn correct alignment, study pranayama, meditation, yoga philosophy, anatomy, and full teaching methodology. Step by step, you build strength in your body, clarity in your mind, and the skills to guide safe and meaningful classes for others. | Pelatihan Guru Yoga 200 Jam kami di Bali adalah kursus menyeluruh tingkat pemula hingga menengah bagi siapa saja yang ingin menjadi guru yoga percaya diri atau memperdalam yoga dalam kehidupan sehari-hari. YogaGarhi berada di Gianyar, sekitar 15 km dari Ubud, menawarkan ketenangan alam pedesaan. Selama kursus, Anda mempraktikkan asana setiap hari, mempelajari anatomi penjajaran, pranayama, meditasi, filosofi yoga, dan metode mengajar. | Our 200 Hour Yoga Teacher Training in Bali is a full, beginner-to-intermediate level course for students who want to become confident yoga teachers or deeply understand yoga for their own life. YogaGarhi is in Gianyar, only about 15 km from Ubud, so you enjoy a peaceful, green village setting close to nature with easy access to Bali’s main yoga and culture center. He is a well-respected and helpful Yoga teacher who leaves a positive and lasting impression on his student's minds.<br/><br/>He strongly believes yoga can be understood only if one is ready to accept the disciplinary rules and regulations one needs to practice and only when those are fulfilled one can experience the rising benefits of yoga in everyday life. | Seamless integration of classical Sanskrit terminology within ID grammatical structure. |
